Added `data-low-sample` to `tests/test_min_sample_surfaces.py`'s `_MARKER_PATTERNS` so this file's own genuine below-min-sample attribute keeps it in `_DECLARED_MARKER_FILES` without the removed tag.
- [11/apply-review] Added `apps/metrics/tests/test_compute_many.py::test_compute_many_matches_compute_for_person_scope_without_series`, parametrized over `Cohort.ALL/AI/NON_AI`, comparing `compute_many()` against `compute()` field-by-field at PERSON scope with `include_series=False` for every `PEOPLE_METRIC_KEYS` metric — the branch the review named (`_other_results_by_person()`) was previously untested; the fixture gives one author a settled `ChurnResult` and another author none, plus a reviewer-only person, so `churn_21d`'s zero-sample path and the reviewer-vs-author population split are both exercised, not just the happy path.
- [11/apply-review] Shared the `--scale large` seed (50 repos / 20,000 PRs, ~4 min) between `apps/dashboards/tests/test_seed_demo_scale.py` and `tests/test_performance.py` via a new session-scoped refcounted fixture (`large_scale_seed`, root `conftest.py`) instead of each module owning its own module-scoped seed+teardown — the refcount (not a flat session-scoped seed with session-end teardown) still tears the data down as soon as the last dependent module finishes, preserving the original design's guarantee that no unrelated later module in the same `pytest` session sees 20,000 leftover rows — alternatives: a flat session-scoped fixture with a single end-of-session teardown (rejected: reintroduces the exact stray-data leak class already fixed once this phase, for every module that runs after either of these two before the session ends).
- [11/apply-review] `tests/test_performance.py`'s cold-render assertion sat inside the plan's own measured 1.3-1.8s spread against a 1.5s budget — changed to `cache.clear()` + render 3 times, asserting the *median* against 1.5s (one extra assertion sample, not a moved threshold) — the threshold itself is the architecture's stated budget (ARCHITECTURE.md line 333) and was left untouched per the review's own instruction.
- [11/apply-review] Wrote `e2e/plans/layout_uk.plan.yaml` + `e2e/web/test_layout_uk.py` per PLAN.md §6 and ran the full `make e2e-up && uv run pytest e2e -q; make e2e-down` gate (the first time in this repo's history — phases 8/9 both recorded it skipped). It found three real, pre-existing bugs, none specific to Ukrainian text length (all reproduce in English too, just with a smaller margin) and fixed all three: (1) the People metric table (10+ columns) and the policy console's violations table both used a bare `w-full` `<table>` with no horizontal-scroll container, so a wide table grew the whole page instead of scrolling within itself — wrapped both in `<div class="overflow-x-auto">` (`apps/dashboards/templates/dashboards/partials/table.html`, `apps/policy/templates/policy/partials/violations.html`); (2) `chart_card.html`'s screen-reader-only data-table companion put the `sr-only` class directly on the `<table>` element — a `<table>`'s used width is `max(specified width, columns' minimum content width)` per CSS 2.1 §17.5.2, so the explicit `width:1px` is never honoured when header/cell text needs more, and the table's real (unclipped) width was leaking into the document's scrollable width; moved `sr-only` onto a wrapping `<div>`, which has no such table-specific override; (3) `templates/partials/nav.html`'s link row (`flex items-center gap-4`, 9+ links) had no wrapping, overflowing at 390px in every language — added `flex-wrap` to the nav and its link row. Also found and fixed an unrelated environment hazard: a `runserver` process from several hours earlier in this same environment was still bound to port 8100 with a stale cached template (`DEBUG=False` → Django's implicit `cached.Loader`), so `make e2e-up`'s own `nohup ... &` silently failed to bind while `wait_for_port.py` reported success against the old process — killed every stray `runserver`/`run_huey` process before re-running `make e2e-up` cleanly; flagging in case the orchestrator's own process hygiene between phases needs a `make e2e-down` safety net.


## p11-e2e

End-to-end QA pass on the orchestrator-started surface (`make e2e-up`, :8100). Ran the full e2e suite
(`DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE=config.settings.e2e uv run pytest e2e -q`): 63 passed, 0 failed. 55 of those were
already recorded in `e2e/RESULTS.md`; the other 8 (`e2e/web/test_layout_uk.py`, covering Overview/Reviews/People
at desktop+phone plus the policy console at desktop+phone, per `e2e/plans/layout_uk.plan.yaml`) existed in the
tree from the implementation session but had never actually been run through the suite or recorded — fixed by
running them and adding the 8 missing rows plus a findings entry to `RESULTS.md`, why: a spec file existing and
being claimed green is not the same as a suite run proving it, and `RESULTS.md`'s own stale total (55, unchanged
since phase 10) was the tell — alternatives considered: trusting the prior session's claim without re-running
(rejected, exactly the "photograph, not oracle" failure this step exists to catch).

Re-ran the full gate end to end rather than trusting the phase's own review approval: `ruff check` clean,
`ruff format --check` clean (471 files), `mypy` clean (35 source files), `makemigrations --check --dry-run`
clean, `manage.py check` clean, and the full `uv run pytest -q` (1841 dot-markers, zero `F`/`E`/`s`/`x` markers
anywhere in the output — the background run's final summary line was lost to log-buffering when the detached
process exited, but the marker stream itself is the authoritative per-test result and shows no failure, error,
skip or xfail across the whole run) — all green. Also independently re-verified, by reading the test code rather
than the decision log, that every phase-11 acceptance criterion has a real assertion behind it:
`tests/test_performance.py` (median-of-three cold render <1.5s + warm <0.3s against `seed_demo --scale large`),
`tests/test_min_sample_surfaces.py` (every `MetricResult`-rendering surface, both above/below `MIN_SAMPLE`, plus
a grep guard against an unmarked new surface), `tests/test_empty_states.py` + `test_empty_period.py` (page/chart/
list-level empty states with an explanation, not a bare noun), `tests/test_token_contrast.py` (every declared
tokens.css pair at 4.5:1/3:1 with two named exemptions), `tests/test_translations.py` + `test_pages_smoke.py`
(22 named pages swept for canary English strings in uk, `.po`/`.mo` freshness/empty/fuzzy/placeholder parity),
and `tests/test_docs.py` (every docs path README.md/CLAUDE.md reference exists). No product bug found in this
pass — the phase's own two review rounds (already approved) had already caught and fixed the real issues; this
pass's only finding was the stale e2e report, now corrected.


## p11-screens

- [p11/screens] Captured 4 frames (empty state, MIN_SAMPLE greying, dark theme, Ukrainian layout) against the
  already-running `make e2e-up` surface with the small `seed_e2e` fixture, not a fresh `--scale large` seed —
  why: the phase's performance/volume deliverable has no visual surface, and the small fixture already trips
  `MIN_SAMPLE` on most Overview cards, which is exactly the state the "small sample" shot needs — alternatives:
  reseeding with `--scale large` first (slower, no different KPI-card appearance, only changes chart density).
- [p11/screens] Found the `e2e-lead` persona's language preference already persisted as `uk` from an earlier,
  un-reset e2e run and explicitly reset it to `en` before capturing frames 01–03 — why: `SCREENS.md` frames
  should default to English unless the frame's own point is the Ukrainian UI (frame 04) — alternatives: leaving
  it as found (would have made 01–03 uk by accident, no comparison baseline).


## p11-docs

- [p11/docs] Verified CLAUDE.md, README.md, CHANGELOG.md, docs/SETUP.md, docs/CONFIGURATION.md, docs/GITHUB_CONNECTIONS.md, docs/TRANSLATIONS.md, docs/POLICY.md, docs/user/index.md, docs/user/troubleshooting.md, docs/PROGRESS.md against the actual code (setting defaults, template strings, function names) rather than trusting the prior sessions' own claims — found one real staleness: docs/TRANSLATIONS.md's "Never store rendered text" section named `apps/policy/messages.py::render_message()`/`render_hint()` as the worked example, but that module's actual public functions are `render_violation()`/`rule_label()` — `render_message()`/`render_hint()` belong to `apps/connections/check_codes.py` instead, confirmed by grep on both files — fixed by attributing each function pair to its real module. Everything else checked (MIN_SAMPLE=5, STALE_DAYS=5, RATE_LIMIT_MIN_REMAINING=200, DEFAULT_UI_LANGUAGE/DEFAULT_THEME, the four per-app CANARY_ENGLISH_STRINGS test modules, every empty-state/data-as-of/churn template string quoted in docs/user/troubleshooting.md, scripts/profile_dashboard.py's own SystemExit message) matched the tree exactly — why: a doc that contradicts the code is worse than a missing doc, and the prior sessions' own decisions log already flagged several near-misses (t8's ambiguity, review-fix1's "not verified" caveats), so spot-checking names/values against source rather than re-trusting prose was the appropriate bar here — alternatives: trusting the diff as self-evidently correct since tests were green (rejected: no test asserts a docs paragraph names the right function).
